Fortunately, the trio was fairly easygoing. After accepting your donut, they left, leaving only you behind, propping your chin up and sighing as you looked at the mechanical body on the ground.

Alright, alright, it seems this armor can't be used for now, right? Try to separate the tangled threads and see if you can take off this mechanical body...... Hey, it seems possible? Anyway, you still managed to pull the old little puppet out, so in the end, this body is still useless—just bigger on the outside. Has its inner strength really gotten stronger because of it?

You were curious about the question, but you weren't in the mood to ask. You thoughtfully hung the armor back on the wall, then carried the still-unconscious Spamton outside.

But as soon as he stepped out of the basement, he woke up and then realized where he was.

“…… Just now, I was still a complete failure, and in the end, those [stupid strings] ...... Spamton buried his head in your shoulder, so his voice sounded muffled.

And you just casually reply, "Well, it's nothing—sometimes, only when fate is manipulated can you ensure your own existence as a character. Who can guarantee their life is truly real and under their control?" ”

"But the act of fighting for it is also worthy of praise...... No matter what, you're still willing to try. Sometimes, having already accepted your fate, you might even give up. "You smiled, stepped aside from the only patrolling butler, and then walked to the entrance.

No one stopped you, so you left smoothly.

"So in the end, you still have to come home with me." You curled your lips into a smile, trying hard to keep calm, trying not to laugh out loud.

“…… And [Five Meals of Barbecue]. "Spamton's voice was still muffled, and you just shrugged.

"Alright, alright, yes, yes." You carried him home. Of course, before that, you should first stop by the barbecue restaurant, or maybe go to the cake shop to pack a piece of cake to take home. It would be a great choice for afternoon tea or a late-night snack......

You put Spamton on the seat opposite, opened the menu, and checked a few—well, you remember what Spamton ordered last time, but you still pushed the menu over for him to choose.

Of course, you can see it! He ordered a lot of vegetables and such, clearly meant for you—by the way, why does Cyber BBQ still have porridge?

You see the thick bowl of porridge in front of you fall into a subtle silence, and in the end, you still drink it with a spoon. But this does make your stomach feel much better. Yes, you were lazy and skipped breakfast—though last time you had some bread, sandwiches, and stuff at home, so I guess Spamton has already eaten it.

After swinging your legs twice, you squinted happily and couldn't help but whisper, "I thought you wouldn't care about me skipping breakfast......

After all, after all this time, you two feel like ordinary roommates? Anyway, you don't really notice any difference.

"How could you think I wouldn't care, or not put in my true heart for it?" You could see the little puppet caught fire again, so you cleared your throat twice.

"Ah...... I thought, actually, you don't like me that much either? Stuff like that. "You gave a couple of awkward laughs and scratched your face. You're not going to fantasize about others actually liking you."

Although she had asked like that on the Ferris wheel before...... maybe she was just overthinking it. Although she said that kissing at the top would mean a lifetime or something, perhaps, naturally separating wouldn't be a big deal.

Think about it, you wouldn't think that's wrong, just be sad for a few days, feel empty for a few days, then smooth out the habit, and then become carefree again.

Just as you were distracted, you suddenly felt your cheek be kissed. You covered your face and stood there in a daze for two seconds, then instinctively glanced to the side and saw Spamton sitting there, his eyes wide open beneath his glasses, staring at you intently. Then he glanced at the empty chair across the street. You reacted slowly—oh, the little puppet had quickly climbed down from the chair and back onto the seat beside you, then kissed you—though it was just standing up and kissing your cheek.

"Oh...... Oh, alright, I get what you mean. "You sighed, scratched your face awkwardly, then turned your face away. After all, you were blushing now, and turning around to look at Spamton would be too awkward! But your face was still turned back by the puppet's little hand. To be honest, you didn't deliberately struggle hard, otherwise he definitely couldn't have done it.

So now you're still staring at Spamton, except this time you're the one blushing.

You covered your mouth and coughed dryly twice, then pretended to be busy. Just then, the meat arrived.

"Ah, the dishes are all here, let's grill them first—if they stay too long, it won't be good!" You muttered to yourself, about to reach for your tools, but then you heard Spamton's voice.

"I am considering that perhaps I need to make another verbal statement, but what I hold is my affection for you."

Your movement stopped.

[7]

After hesitating for a while, you stopped what you were doing and then lowered your head to look at the little puppet beside you.

"So, why do you like me?" That's probably the question you're most curious about right now.

But Spamton just turned his head away: "[No comment.] ”

Well, it seems he really doesn't want to tell you, though you can guess why he turned away...... It's probably just because he's shy about something, but since he doesn't want to talk, you won't force him to do it—you're very understanding.

"Well, fine, if you won't tell me, then so be it." So you just shrugged, then turned around and started grilling the meat with full concentration.

Spamton, who had been waiting for you to ask more, paused and turned to look at you with an expression that was almost incredulous.

That kind of gaze is like a thorn in your back. You really can't stand it anymore, so you turn around and glare at him: "Why are you always staring at me!" ”

Come to think of it, this isn't the first or second time. You've always felt this way before—you felt something was off, but after realizing it was just Spamton's gaze, you deliberately ignore it, so when you're stared at, you feel a strange sense of familiarity.

But this time, the gaze was so intense that it couldn't be ignored, and for some reason, it carried a sense of condemnation......

You pause, take off the freshly grilled slices of meat, then tentatively ask Spamton, "Do you want me to keep asking?" ”

Spamton didn't answer, just kept looking at you.

Alright, alright, let's just leave it at that. You take a deep breath and ask again: "Then let me ask again, why do you actually like me?" ”

"The answer is still [No comment]." For some reason, he looked a bit smug, tilting his head and landing on him in a somewhat eerie way...... Cuteness.

You were out of options, clutching your hair hard: "Wow, you did it on purpose, didn't you?! Actually, you just wouldn't tell me the answer or anything!" ”

Even if you don't really want to know the answer, that's all.

You turn around angrily and sit a bit further inside, basically even farther from Spamton. You don't want to see him for now—break up...... Forget it, just cut ties for three minutes.

But just as you moved over, you heard Spamton's voice again: "I didn't mean to, but I think the mood of 'born from the heart' shouldn't be something that can be casually summarized or redescribed." ”

Well, you keep making it again, and you calm your breath a little. Although now you're thinking inappropriately—can a puppet have a heart? You're curious about this question, but out of politeness, you still won't ask.

"Mm-hmm, I know, but I'm still angry. If you won't tell me, then don't. Why do you want me to ask you again?" You turned your head and grabbed Spamton's face, shaking him from side to side.

Well, anyway, it's not really as hard as a puppet; if you want to pull it, you can still pull it. So you just grabbed Spamton's face, making him a bit slurred.

"That also seems ...... Your [freedom]. ”

“…… I shouldn't have asked you." You pressed your temples and turned your head again. To be honest, I should let you calm down for a while. Maybe you'll be fine soon.

After a moment of silence, you started drinking the bowl of porridge in front of you. To be honest, after the recent conversation, the bowl had gotten a bit cold, but for you, it was enough to calm your anger.

After a moment of silence, you feel your arm being poked again. You glance to the side and realize Spamton has somehow moved over.

He looked like he had something to say. You sighed but still asked, "So, what else do you want to say?" ”

"I want to make another [Trade]." He said this, lowering his head so you couldn't see his expression at this moment.

"Hmm, so what's the deal this time?" You're a bit curious, since you already refused his deal last time. What is he going to propose this time?

“…… I want to use my [Love] to make a [Trade] and gain your [Love]. "Spamton said.

You paused, searched through your mind, and found something you'd said some time ago—well, that night you did say that liking and loving are different to you, and what you have now is just liking.

You stick out your tongue: "Is that so...... Honestly, I don't really know what love means to me. ”

You never really understood this, only vaguely sensing that there should be a difference between the two. Otherwise, you would love too many people!

"To me, liking someone probably just means it's fun, just because I find it fun and brings me joy. I don't care what the other person thinks; I'm just considering my own feelings. Of course, I'm even willing to do something, anyway—well, that's why I wanted to drag you out to play before." You coughed twice as you spoke.

After all, you're just afraid of loneliness.

"Besides, what you were thinking at the time isn't really important to me. This kind of fondness is more innocent than for a person, just treating something that can be enjoyed for fun." Lowering your gaze, you muttered to yourself. Fortunately, Spamton didn't speak at that moment, probably just listening carefully to your words.

"But...... But! What exactly does love count as? Is it caring about the other person's feelings, giving everything for them? Or is it forcing your own obsessions and love on the other person according to your own will? Or is it enough to calmly see the other person happy and happy? Perhaps it's just a concept...... Maybe it's a heartfelt, responsible, and infinitely courageous emotion? ”

You shake your head and finish reading this passage, then look at Spamton. You blink and ask seriously, "So, which one do you want?" ”

But Spamton just shook his head, as if to say he didn't want either.

You don't really know what he needs, so you just sigh and extend your right hand: "Alright, but I can agree to this deal—but you have to tell me what kind of love you really need, or, if you don't want to do it, at least teach me how to love you." ”

By the end, you start to feel uncertain, since you don't know if this counts as a valid deal, and you might even feel a bit guilty.

But you only see Spamton extend his right hand, then rest it on yours, and hear his voice: "...... [Deal]." ”

"Oh!" Alright, it's settled then. But let's discuss this later. For now, let's have some barbecue! "You happily withdrew your hand, then clapped, and placed the little puppet on your lap. After all, you two are already in this relationship...... Uh, anyway, let's just give up on those etiquette rules; it's not that important.

When you finish eating and go to pay, the waiter still looks at Spamton with that pretty boy gaze, and also with a heartbroken gaze. You guess she probably means, why did you bring this pretty boy over to eat with you again?

Maybe they're still urging you to give up early and switch to another one, but think about it—you probably never will. A pretty boy is just a pretty boy! Worst case, I'll do his makeup next time I go out!

You happily made the decision and carried Spamton out the door.

"So, can you [place] me on the ground?" Staying in your arms with no touch to the ground made him afraid to struggle, so he had no choice but to ask for your opinion.

You thought for a moment, then put him down on the ground: "Alright, anyway, we'll just walk slowly for a while, and we'll be home soon." ”

By the way, you grabbed Spamton's outstretched hand, and this time he actually took the initiative to reach out.

Although you may not be entirely sure what these subtle changes mean...... But in any case, it's just getting better little by little!

After that, you returned home as usual, and this time you truly began living together. To you, it was more like roommates sharing a room.

Although you used to sleep with a puppet, you still do it now. The only difference is that now you give Spamton's head a kiss every day while you sleep.

This has become a habit, and if one day you can't do this, you might feel quite disappointed—well, maybe you shouldn't even make that kind of thought.

But with it comes a lot of trouble, because you're basically a person with no basic life sense, stockpiling a bunch of frozen pizza, steak, and all sorts of buns in the fridge. The eggs have grown mold before and haven't been thrown away, and when some things need to be replaced at home, you don't bother buying them and just go through the motions. Everything at home is a mess.

Then you just squatted there in silence, not daring to speak, watching Spamton tiptoe and throw away all your hairy eggs, then made you carry him to buy groceries—well, you behaved obediently.

He can even cook! Even though you still need to hold it, and the dish doesn't look very good, it actually tastes quite good—in short, much better than you. You eat the bowl of pasta in front of you, tears in your eyes. Yes, you can only cook noodles obediently.

But this kind of life has settled down, even more peaceful than before. You've gradually gotten used to the feeling of letting others share most of the burden—you'll be the one to pay, and as for other goals, we'll deal with them later.

On this day, you sat in the cake shop. After all, Spamton had promised to owe you five cakes, even though what he actually used was still your money—you were just giving him money.

You poke the matcha cake in front of you with your fork, gently lift your eyes to look at the little puppet sitting across from you, and tilt your head slightly.

“…… I wonder, should this kind of feeling be considered love? No matter what, will it always be like this from now on? ”

For once, you felt a bit nervous, just lowered your head and unconsciously poked the cake with your fork, then you saw Spamton's shadow appear on the table and nod.

So you smile happily, then call the waiter to serve Spamton a matcha cake.

"Okay, one for each of us."

We're just friends with benefits

As the title suggests, this is an R18 story where both the plot and the story take off (. ）

This SPT is set during the period of big shots, and after becoming a small person

In short, OOC counts me as well. This story contains some Russian writing style and Japanese-style stomachache emotional developments, plus some three-part black mud plot points. The ending might feel a bit rushed.

Prequel

[1]

…… To be honest, you've completely wandered into another world! To be honest, you were just curious...... After all, you discovered that in a remote alley you happened to pass through, there was actually a door, and it could be opened. Even if that door opened and it was just pitch black. But out of curiosity to find out...... Or maybe you were just in a bad mood, and since you were already here, you decided to go and see.

Whatever it is, you just left a suffocating home, but you know you'll come back sooner or later. So even if you escape, you won't run completely, because besides that, you have nowhere to go, and no one needs you. But no matter what, even if you suddenly fall into an abyss, even if you might suddenly die, it's all good.

But there were none of those things—only the howling wind, a sudden dizziness, and a flash of intense light that made you close your eyes involuntarily. When you snapped back to reality and opened your eyes again, you found yourself standing on another street.

You stared blankly, instinctively trying to walk forward. It was a deep and dark blue. For some reason, there was almost no light around, just a dim atmosphere. It wasn't until you felt the first rain on your face, followed by even more raindrops, pouring so hard it almost soaked your whole body. Only then did you panic and realize, you instinctively turned around to try to pull the door, but for some reason, it wouldn't open now.

…… Filled with anxiety and unease, you covered your head with your hand and ran under the nearest eaves.

Then you started to worry, just staring blankly at the rain before you. The pouring rain grew heavier, and the continuous rain curtain made everything before you hazy and hazy. Here, you could see some neon lights, probably even the flickering signs hanging above buildings, but you couldn't make out what words were written on them. Through the chaotic rain, the neon lights became blurry and distorted. You could vaguely make out figures on the street, but they were different from anything you'd seen before. If you had to describe them, it would be more likely...... Purely living creatures? That's pretty novel.

But you have no umbrella, and with such heavy rain, you have nowhere to go. This is just a strange world, so you can only endure the moisture in the air and the gradually dropping temperature, waiting alone for the rain to stop. You clasp your arms tightly, lost and helpless, the only glimmer in your eyes extinguished, just quietly staying there.

Spamton saw you at that moment. To be honest, he just walked out of the shop as usual, watching the heavy rain outside, intending to pull an umbrella from the nearby umbrella rack, but then he stopped.

Unlike before, the street across the street was not empty or gray; instead, there was a figure here. Perhaps it was a passing car flashing its flash, illuminating your eyes, while you only closed your eyes and shrank back in discomfort, with droplets still clinging to your cheeks—it was unclear whether it was rain or tears.

He instinctively swallowed hard, and for some reason, he felt as if a fire was burning in his chest. Then, even though he was just standing in such a cold environment, he still felt a burning heat.

He didn't know what he was thinking at that moment; all Spamton knew was that he had an impulse to lick the droplets off his cheek, then bite your lips, and then ......

He didn't think any further. He paused and resumed his movements, but his hand changed direction. Originally, he wanted to use a larger umbrella because he didn't want the rain to soak his suit. But now, he picked up a small umbrella nearby, which could barely cover a person. After all, it was a sunshade umbrella and wasn't very spacious by nature.

He held up the umbrella, walked through the rain, and walked toward you, curling his lips into a familiar smile that was most comfortable and reassuring: "Miss, I see you staying here because of the troubles caused by the heavy rain? How about I [hold the umbrella] and send you off? Sharing an umbrella is always better than a damp ending." ”

You had been daydreaming at first, but when you heard that, you finally snapped back to reality. Honestly, although there seemed to be a bit of a pause and something odd in the middle, the voice actually sounded pretty pleasant. And it sounded quite polite...... In short, your previously listless mood improved a bit.

Turning your head, you see a ...... At least someone who really does look like a person is standing there holding an umbrella, reaching out a hand toward you, probably signaling you to put it on it.

You hesitated for a moment, a bit uneasy: "Wouldn't that be too much trouble for you? I'll just wait here for the rain to stop, no need for you to see me off......"

…… And it's just a chance encounter—why help you? You can't help him at all, you have nothing to express gratitude to, and if he feels guilty, it's better never to have done it at all.

So you just shake your head and put on a distant smile—well, maybe it's pretty stiff, since you're not used to smiling at all. Even though your parents often say you have to smile to make everyone happy and happy yourself, you've only learned to smile mechanically.

This is the first time you've tried to make a different expression. From the stiff corners of your mouth, you can sense that you have probably failed.

Spamton naturally noticed your somewhat stiff expression, but he said nothing, just continued reaching out his hand on his own: "It's not [troublesome], I'm very happy to [serve you]." ”

…… Even if you say that, what do you think in your heart? Maybe it's a different approach? Thinking this, you stared blankly at the hand he reached out, then at his still smiling face, tilted your head, and asked, "Uh...... Sir, we have never met. Why do you want to help me? Maybe there won't be any reward at all. ”

"I don't [need] [payment], nor do I have many [reasons]. I just [hope] to provide [help] to [you]." He was still very patient. In fact, he had thought you might be very cautious, and had thought you might reject him once or twice, but definitely not a third time. Spamton was still confident in this.

You take a deep breath and look seriously into his eyes this time, but you can't see them. He has his eyes closed, just squinting and smiling, so you can't tell what emotions he's really feeling. Or maybe you can't understand other people's emotions either.

“…… Then why me? "You murmured, to be honest, you don't know what answer would satisfy you, or maybe you just want to hear an answer. Whatever it is, it doesn't matter.

You hear him chuckle: "It's just because it's [you]." ”

You stopped speaking, but for some reason, tears slid down your eyes. You didn't intend to care anymore. Maybe this sudden downpour was just like your mood—sad and endless, you didn't know where to go, and you had nowhere to go.

…… Maybe you just want to be chosen once, or maybe you just want to know you still have a reason to stand here.

So in the end, you still held his hand and softly said, "Thank you for your trouble. ”

Watching the tears dripping down your cheeks, Spamton's throat swallowed again; he could clearly hear the sound of swallowing. To be honest, he really wanted to kiss the tears on your cheeks, then kiss your lips, and then swallow everything—whether sweet or bitter, he would accept it with a smile. But he also knew he absolutely couldn't do that now, or else you'd definitely leave far away.

After hearing this, Spamton's smile deepened a little, becoming even more genuine. He always knew, it's impossible to refuse this deal more than three times...... No, this isn't even a transaction, it's just a gift. As for the reward for the transaction, it's naturally repaid after the debt is owed.

"In that case, 'ladies first', I'll make sure [you] try not to be attacked by [Rain]." As he said this, he stretched the umbrella over your head, leaving most of his body exposed.

…… The suit can be changed later; what's more important now is the situation. He doesn't want to lose you in a flash and never find you again. It's better to seize the opportunity and make sure you remember him forever.

You looked blankly at the umbrella hanging over your head. To be honest, it was very small—even if just one person held it in such heavy rain, it would be a struggle, so you couldn't help but feel a bit guilty. Besides, you helped out for free, so I shouldn't have let you get wet......

You're really embarrassed. If you hadn't already walked a distance, you'd want to run right under the eaves right now. He only needed to hold an umbrella for himself.

"No, I'm serving you. As long as you're comfortable, that's all that matters." Of course, you didn't push the umbrella; you just shook it, and a string of raindrops poured down from the tip, soaking the clothes on his shoulder. You didn't dare move at all.

“…… Alright, then if you want to make me feel better, I'm too embarrassed to see you getting wet in the rain......" you whispered, then you saw only innocence in his eyes, and he didn't respond to your words. In any case, you hesitated for a moment, but still gritted your teeth and closed the distance between you.

You don't really like interacting with the opposite sex; you just instinctively resist it, but it's not impossible. So now you still feel that restless feeling as you close the distance, and you feel your shoulders bumping into each other as you walk. To be honest, you're not used to this.

And although it had improved a bit, you could still see half of his body exposed. The rain was just a bit lighter than before, so it didn't look so scary.

“…… Maybe you should just hold an umbrella and take me somewhere to stand in the rain. I'm really embarrassed." You muttered.

"[No need], I will send you to an excellent [home]. And it's not [far]. The [destination] is just around the corner. "And Spamton simply said this.

You hesitated for a moment. There really wasn't much place nearby to take shelter from the rain. This area was all brick walls, not a single eave, and you weren't familiar with the roads of the other world, so you didn't know if he was leading you to some remote road. But there were still cars passing by on the street, and you could see pedestrians passing by, so it was probably just a less busy street.

In the end, after hesitating for a moment, you still crawled in front of him, though it was a bit farther than when you were in your arms and close to your chest. You felt you couldn't do that, probably just a hint of intuition...... You didn't know what harm it would bring.

But now, maintaining that posture, your body feels a bit stiff, so you quicken your pace a bit, since you don't want to bump into his footsteps. The umbrella moves along your head, so now it covers both of you. You feel his hand lightly resting on your shoulder, probably not wanting to slip up all at once.

Maybe it was the inexplicable awkwardness, but in any case, both of you fell silent. No one spoke anymore, and you just quickened your pace and moved forward.

Spamton only watched as you occasionally showed the fair skin on your neck from your hurried steps, and at your white dress, which had become a bit damp and transparent from the raindrops on your earlier haste. His thoughts were a bit chaotic. In the end, he chose to look away a little, or else all the heat would probably have gathered together.

I don't know how much time had passed, or maybe just a moment, but it was indeed quite close, because you were standing under the eaves, with a residence in front of you. Then you watched as Spamton took out his keys, opened the door in front of him, and gestured invitingly toward you.

"SPAMTON G. SPAMTON, if you need compensation services, maybe...... Add [Contact Information]? ”

So your voice grew even softer: "...... Ah, I don't have a phone." You feel like you're about to cry again, so you lower your head.

You feel his voice seem a bit closer to you: "Maybe I can [help] [you] [buy a phone]." ”

The residents of the [Light World] where [you] are the [People of Light], while the inhabitants of the [Dark World] are the [People of Darkness]. ”

You nodded half-understandingly, half understanding, probably understanding it, but you still had some doubts: "So the Dark People need to help the Light Tribes?" Why? Aren't they just residents of two worlds? There shouldn't be any contact, right? ”

Of course, saying this doesn't mean he's so willing to serve other people of light—in fact, he believes that whether it's people of light or darkness, for him it's only mutually beneficial. If they can't be used, it's fine to turn a blind eye; if they can't provide value, there's no need to offer convenience.
